ELECTION COMMISSION OF INDIA 

ORDERS 

New Delhi, the 18th July 1970 
S . O . 2758. — Whereas the Election Commission is satisfied that Shil Nitya 
Ranjan Das, Village Baikunthapur, P . O . Triboni, District Hooglily , West Bengal 
a contesting candidate for the mid - term election held in February , 1969 , to the 
West Bengal Legislative Assembly from 175 , Chinsurah Constituency , has failed to 
lodge an account of his election expenses as required by the Representation of the 
People Act, 1951, and the Rules made thereunder ; 

And whereas the said candidate , even after due notices , has not glven any 
reason or explanation for the failure and the Election Commission is satisfied that 
he has no good reason or justification for the failure ; 

Now , therefore, in pursuance of section 10A of the said Act , the Election Com 
mission hereby declares the said Shri Nitya Ranjan Das to be disqualifed for 
being chosen as, and for being , a member of either House of Parliament or of the 
Legislative Assembly or Legislative Council of a State for a period of three years 
from the date of this order 


No. WB-LA / 175 / 69 ( 35 ). 


( 3659 )

एस० ० 2758. - - पत.निर्वाचन आयोग का सनाधान हो गया है कि पश्चिमी बंगाल विधान 
सभा के लिए फरवरी 1969 में हुए मध्यावधि निर्वाचन के लिए 175 चिनसुरा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से चुनाव 
लड़ने वाले उम्मीदवार श्री नित्यारंज । दास ग्राम बैकुंजर पो० - -त्रिवेनी जिला - हुगली ( पश्चिमी 
बंगाल ) लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम 1951 तथा तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित अपने निर्वाचन 
व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं ; 

__ और यतः उक्त उम्मीदवार ने उस मन्त्रक सूचना दिए जाने पर भी अपनी इम अनफलता के 
लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण नही दिया है तथा निर्वाचन आयोग का यह सनाधान हो गया है 
कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या न्यायोचित्य नही है ; 

प्रत : अब उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10 - 3 के अनुसरण में निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा उक्त 
श्री नित्यारंजन दास को संसद के दोनों सदनों में से किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान सभा 
अथवा विधान परिषद के सदस्य व ने जाने और होने के लिए इस आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्षकी काला 
पाधि के लिए निहित घोषित करता है । 

[ सं० १०-ब० वि० म० / 175/ 69 ( 35 ) .]

tta Ipdge an account of his election expenses as required by the Representation of

And whereas the said candidate , even after due notices , has not given any

ए१० प्रो० 2759. - - - तः निर्वाचन आयोग का समाधान हो गया है कि पश्चिमी बंगाल विधान 
सभा के लिए फरवरी 1969 में हए मध्यावधि निर्वाचन के लिए 172 ---चन निर्वचन-क्षेत्र 
से च नाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीदवार श्री गौर गोपाल बनी चम्पाटाला, पो० - चन्द्रनगर जिला हुगली 
( पश्चिमी बं ल ) लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम 1951 तथा तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित 
अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं ; 


और यतः उक्त उम्मीदवार ने उसे सम्यक सूचना दिए जाने पर भी अपनी इस असफलता 
के लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण नही दिया है ; तथा निर्वाचन अ य ग का रह ममाध न हो गया 
है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या न्यायौचित्य नही है ;

ORDERS 

New Delhi, the 20th July 1970 
S . O . 2760. — Whereas the Election Commission is satisfied that Shri Qamarud 
din, S / o . Shri Mohammad Safi, R / o . village Chauridanopatti Post Office Parst 
pur, District Varanasi, Uttar Pradesh , a contesting candidate for mid - term general 
election to the Uttar Pradesh Legislative Assembly from 253-Gyanpur Assembly 
Constituency , has failed to lodge an account of his election expenses as required 
by the Representation of the People Act , 1951, and the Rules made thereunder ; 

2 . And whereas the said candidate even after due notices has not given any 
reason or explanation for the failure and the Election Commission is satisfied that 
he has no good reason or justification for such failure ; 

3 . Now , therefore , in pursuance of section 10A of the said Act, the Election 
Commission hereby declares the said Shri Qamaruddin , to be disqualified for 
being chosen as and for being a member of either House of Parliament or of the 
Legislative Assembly or Legislative Council of a State for a period of three years 
from the date of this order . 

[ No. UP- LA/ 253/ 69 ( 62 ).] 
प्रादेशों 

नई दिल्ली, 20 जुलाई 1970 
ए० प्रो० 2760. - यतः निर्वाचन आयोग का समाधान हो गया है कि उत्तर प्रदेश विधान 
सभा के लिए मध्यावधि निर्वाचन के लिए 253 -शानपुर सभा निर्वाचन- क्षेत्र से चुनाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीद 
यार श्री कमरुद्दीन सुपुत्र श्री मोहम्मद सकी निवासी गांव चौरीदान पट्टी पो० परसीपुर जिला वाराणसी 

उत्तर प्रदेश लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम 1951 तथा तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित अपने 
निर्वाचन व्ययों का कोई भी लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं ; 

और यतः उक्त उम्मीदवार ने उसे सम्यक सूचना दिए जाने पर भी अपनी इस असफलता के 
लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण नही दिया है तथा निर्वाचन आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया 
है कि उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या न्यायौचित्य नहीं है ; 


प्रतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10 - क के अनुसरण में निर्वाचन प्रायोग एतदद्वारा 
उक्त श्री कमरुद्दीन को संसद के दोनों सदनों में से किसी भी सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान सभा अथवा 
विधान परिषद के सदस्य चने जाने और होने के लिए इस आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि 
के लिए निरहित घोषित करता है । 

[ सं० उ० प्र० -वि० स०/ 253/ 69 ( 62) ]

एस० प्रो० 2761. - --यतः निर्वाचन आयोग का समाधान हो गया है कि उत्तर प्रदेश विधान 
सभा के लिए मध्यावधि निर्वाचन के लिए 253- ज्ञानपुर सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र में चुनाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीद 
वार श्री मोहम्मद हमन सुपुत्र श्री अलीबक्स निवासी गांव पयागदामपुर डा० राधास्वामी धाम जिला 
वाराणसी उत्तर प्रदेश लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम 1951 तथा तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा 
अपेक्षित अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का कोई भी लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं ; 

और यतः उक्त उम्मीदवार ने , उसे सम्यक सूचना दिए जाने पर भी अपनी इस असफलता 
के लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण नहीं दिया है तथा निर्वाचन आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया 
है कि उसके पास इस अमफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या न्यायौचित्य नहीं है ; 

अतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10 - 2 के अनुसरण में निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा उक्त 
श्री मोहम्मद हसन को ससद के दोनों सदनों में से किमी भी सदन के या किमी राज्य की विधान सभा 
अथवा विधान परिषद् के सदस्य चुने जाने और होने के लिए , इस आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की 
कालावधि के लिए निरहित घोषित करता है । 

[ मं० उ० प्र० - वि० स० / 253/ 69( 63 ) .]

एस० प्रो० 2763. - - यत : निर्वाचन आयोग का समाधान हो गया है कि उत्तर प्रदेश विधान 
सभा के लिए निर्वाचन के लिए 95 - भगवन्त नगर निर्वाचन- क्षेत्र से चुनाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीदवार 
श्री चन्द्रपाल सिह सुपुत्र श्री रामेश्वर, निवासी गांव व डा० चमियानी, जिला उन्नाव , उत्तर प्रदेश , 
लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 तथा तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों 
का कोई भी लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं ; 


और यतः, उक्त उम्मीदवार ने , उसे सम्यक सूचना दिए जाने पर भी अपनी इस असफलता 
के लिए कोई कारण अथवा स्पष्टीकरण नहीं दिया है ; तथा निर्वाचन आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया 
है कि उमके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या न्यायौचित्य नहीं है ; 

अतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10 - क के अनुसरण में निर्वाचन आयोग एतदद्वारा उक्त

के लिए निरहित घोपित करता है ।

(Department of Health ) 

ORDER 


New Delhi, the 6th August 1970 
S . 0 . 2176. - Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the late 
Ministry of Health No . 16 - 8 / 61 - MI , dated the 14th August , 1962 the Central 
Government has directed that the Medical qualification , " Doctor of Medicine" 
granted by the University of Hamburg, Germany shall be recognised medical 
qualification for the purposes of the Indian Medical Council Act, 1956 ( 102 of 
1958 ) ; 

And whereas Dr. Abdul Sahib Al-Diwany , who possesses the said qualification 
is for the time being attached to the Ramakrishna Mission Sevashram , Kankhal, 
Hardwar , Uttar Pradesh for the purposes of charitable work ; 

Now , therefore, in pursuance of clause ( c ) of the proviso to sub - section ( 1 ) 
of section 14 of the said Act, the Central Government hereby specifies : 
(i) & period of two years from the date of publication of this order in the 

Official Gazette, or 
(ii) the period during which Dr. Abdul Sahib Al- Diwany is attached to the 

said Ramakrishna Mission Sevashram , Kankhal, 
whichever is shorter , as thc period to which the medical practice by the aforesaid 
doctor shall be limited .

एस०प्रो० 2778. -- इसके साथ लगी प्रथम अनुसूची में निर्धारित प्रत्येक अधिनियम 
के उपबन्ध के अन्तर्गत जारी किये गये निदेशों के अनुसार, केन्द्रीय सरकार, फिल्म सलाहकार बोर्ड 
बम्बई की सिफारिशों पर विचार करने के बाद , एतद्द्वारा, इसके साथ लगी द्वितीय अनुसूची के कालम 
2 में दी गई फिल्म को उसके सभी भाषाओं के रूपान्तरों सहित, जिसका विवरण उसके सामने 
उक्त द्वितीय अनुसूची के कालम 6 में दिया हया है, स्वीकृत करती है :

अधिनियम की धारा 5 की उपधारा ( 3 ) तथा धारा 9 ।

एस० प्रो० 2781 - - सर्व श्री मद्रास पैमिल , फैक्ट्री, मद्रास - 2, को 38, 500 ( अड़तीस हजार पाँच 
सौ रुपये मात्र ) :पये का आयात लाइसेंम संख्या पी० / डी / 21734741 - दिनांक 21 - 1 - 70 , 
सामान्य मुद्रा क्षेत्र मे, पमिल लेट के प्रायान के लिए स्वीकृत किया गया था । उन्होने उपर्युक्त 
लाइसेंम की अनुलिपि ( मीमा शुल्क तथा मुद्रा नियंत्रण अनुलिपि प्रतियां ) जारी करने के लिए प्रावेदन 
किया है , इसके लिए उन्होंने यह प्राधार दिया है कि मूल लाइसेम उनके द्वारा खो गया है । उन्होंने 
प्रागे यह बताया है कि लाइमेंस का बिल्कुल भी उपयोग नहीं किया गया है । लाइमम किमी 
सीमा -शल्क कार्यालय में पंजीकृत नहीं किया गया है । 

इस तर्क के समर्थन में , आवेदक ने एक शपथ -पत्र जमा किया है । मैं संतुष्ट हूं कि मूल 
लाइसेस मं० पी / डी0 2173474, दिनाक 21 - 1 - 1970 खो गया है और निदेश देता हूं कि 
उपयुक्त लाइमेंस ( मीमा शुल्क तथा मा नियंत्रण दोनो ) की अनुलिपि प्रतियां उन्हें जारी की 
जानी चाहिए । मूल लाइमेंस ( दोनों प्रतियां ) रद्द किया जाता है । 
लाइसेम की अनुलिपि प्रतियां अलग से जारी की जा रही हैं ।

AWARD 
The Central Government was of the opinion that an industrial dispute existed 
between the employers in relation to the management of North , South and West 
Jhagrakhand Collierles and their workmen , By their Notification No. 1 / 17 / 68 -LR II 
dated 24th February , 1970 , the Central Government referred to this Tribunal for 
adjudication the following matter in dispute as stated in the schedule to the order 
of reference : - 

SCHEDULE 
" Whether the action of the management of North , South and West Jhagra 

khand Collieries of Messrs Jhagrakhand Collieries (Private ) Limited , 
Post Office Jhagrakhand Colliery, District Surguja (Madhya Pradesh ) 
in denying payment of underground allowance on holiday wage to 
their workmen is justified ? If not to what relief are the workmen 

concerned entitled ? 
2 . After the reference parties fled their written statements rejoinders and 
seven issues were framed by my predecessor. Sri G . P . Sharma was thereafter 
examined as W . W . 1 and after workers closed their evidence . Then the manage 
ment took time to produce its witnesses . The dispute has now been compromised 
as stated in the compromiso petition reproduced in the annexure . The management 
has agreed to give Underground Allowance on wages for paid festival holidays to 
those workmen who are getting Underground Allowance . The workers on their 
part bave given up their demand for payment of arrears of Underground Allowance 
on holiday wages for the period prior to 1st January 1970 and the management 
has agreed to pay it with effect from 1st January 1970 . This compromise shall not 
prejudice or affect the respective stand of the parties in the case relating to Refer 
ence Case No. CGIT / LC ( R ) (24) / 1968 and wļll not be cited as a precedent. The 


Sec. 3 ( ii )] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 22 , 1970 / SRAVANA 31, 1892 3679 


settlement appears to be fair and reasonable and to the advantage of the workmen . 
It is, therefore , accepted and an award is recorded in terms thereof, a copy 01 
which is annexed to the awara .

$ . 0 , 2785 . In pursuance of section 17 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 ( 14 of 
1947), the Central Government hereby publishes the folloning award of the Central 
Government Industrial Tribunal (No. 3 ) , Dhanbad , in the industrial dispute between 
the employers in relation to the management of Bokharo Jharla Colliery of Messrs 
Agarwalla Brothers , Post Office Karmatand, District Dhanbad and their workmen , 
which was received by the Central Government on the 5th August, 1970 . 


CENTRAL GOVERNMENT INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL NO . 3, AT DHANBAD 

REFERENCE NO. 28 OF 1969 
PRESENT : 

Shri Sachidanand Sinha , M . A .M .L ., Presiding Officer .

Vs. 


Their workmen .

Union and Sri P . Chaudhury , Advocate, 
INDUSTRY ; Coal 

STATE : Bihar 
Dhanbad , dated the 21st of July 1970 

AWARD 
1. The Central Government, being of opinion that an Industrial dispute exists 
between the employers in relation to the management of Bokharo Jharia Colliery 
of Messrs Agarwalla Brothers , Post Office Karmatand , District Dhanbad and their 
workmen , by its order No. 2 / 46 / 69 -LRII dated the 19th of April, 1969 referred to 
this Tribunal under section 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 for ad 
judication the dispute in respect of the matters specified in the schedule annexed 
thereto . The Schedule is extracted below : 

SCHEDULE 
" Whether the management of Bokharo Jharla Colllery of Messrs Agarwalla 

Brothers , Post Onice Karmatand , Distiict Dhanbad , refused work to 
Sri Shambhu Mahato , General Mazdoor with effect from the 8th 
November , 1968 ? If so , to what relief is the workman concerned 

entitled ? 
2 . The Vice President, Bihar Colliery Kamgar Union Aled the written statement 
on behalf of the workmen on 14th August 1969. Their case is that the concerned 
workman Sri Shambhu Mahato was in the employment in the said Bokharo Jharja 
Colliery of Messrs Agarwalla Brothers since 1948 and worked continuously as a 
General Mazdoor in the said colliery till 7th Noveinber , 1988 . 

3 . The concerned workman Sri Shambhu Mahato was a permanent employee . 
but he was never paid his proper wages. He was never paid any quarterly 
bonus, profit sharing bonus, leave wages . In spite of the fact that he was a 
member of Coal Mines Provident Fund ( a / e . No . D / 282431 ) but evcept for some 
time in the year , 1956 -57 no deduction was made towards his Provident Fund 
Contribution nor the management paid their share of Provident Fund contribu 
tion . The concerned workman requested the management to pay him proper 
wages, quarterly bonus , lcave wages and profit sharing bonus. But he was threa 
tened that he would be discharged from service , if he persister! In demanding 
proper wages , leave wages, quarterly bonus etc The concerned workman , being 
in fear of losing his services and even half bread which he was getting , kept quiet 
and did not know what to do . 

4 . The concerned workman became member of Blhar Colliery Kamgar Union , 
Dhanbad in the year 1968 Thereafter the concerned workman Aled A 
case under section 33 - C ( 2 ) of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 before the Central 
Government Labour Court No. 2 , at Dhanbad on 29th October , 1968 , being L . C . 
case No. 509 of 1968 , claiming difference of wages , quarterly bonus, profit sharing 


Sec . 3 (11 )] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA . AUGUST 22, 1970 /SRAVANA 31, 1892 3681 


- 


- 


- 


" 


bonus, leave wages and the management s share of Coal Mines Provident contribu 
tion As soon as the management of the said Bokharo Jharia Colllery, received 
the notice of this L , C . Case No 509 of 1968 from the Central Government Labour 
Court No 2 , at Dhanbad , the concerned workman was called by the Proprietor of 
the colliery , Sri Hari Ram Agarwalla and was told by him that as he had Aled 
this case, he was no longer to work in the col lery and thus relused him work 
with ellect from 8th November, 1968 . The action oť the management of Bokharo 
Jharia Colliery is quite illegal, unjustified and is an act of victimisation On these 
grounds the union prayed that the concerned workinan be reinstated with full back 
wages and other benefits and with continuity of service 

5 The employers Aled their written statement on 27th May , 1969, and their 
rejoinder on 17th November , 1969 Their case is that the concerned workman Sri 
Sambhu Mahato , was employed at the colliery in the year 1956 and has not been 
working there at any time after 1957, and in this view of the case the question of 
refusal of work does not arise . 

6 Shri Shambhu Mahato had applied for the refund of his accumulations in 
the Coal Mines Provident Fund, in the month of March 1968 The accumula 
tions in the provident fund are payable only on permanent retirement from service 
or on retirement on account of certain grounds and the office of the Commissioner , 
Coal Mines Provident Fund Dhanbad by letters dated 19th March , 1988 and 20 / 21st 
June, 1968, required of the management certain particulars in respect of the above 
workman , to settle his claim for refund of accumulations in the Coal Mines 
Provident Fund The Alling of application in March , 1968 , for the refund of 
accumulation of the Provident Fund would give le to the claim that Shri Sham 
bhu Mahato was refused or stopped from work on the 8th of November , 1968 
The management also denied that the concerned workman was stopped from his 
duties by order of Shri Hari Ram Agarwalla after receiving notice of the case 
No 509 of 1988 The concerned workman was not in the employment at the 
relevant time and as such the question of refusing employment to him from 8th 
November , 1968, does not arise 

7 On behalf of the workman two witnesses were examined viz , WW - 1 Sri 
Shambhu Mahato , the concerned workman and WW - 2 Santi Mahato son of the 
concerned workman 8 items of documents were exhibited on behalf of the work 
men and they are marked Ext W - 1 to W - 8 On behalf of the employers two 
witnesses were examined viz , MW - 1 Sri Hari Prasad Kumar Bonus clerk and 
MW - 2 Sri Manik Chandra Sarkar, Compounder in the hospital of the Bokharo 
Jharia Colliery 13 Items of documents were also exhibited on behalf of the 
employers and they are marked Ext M1 to M - 13 

8 The point for consideration is whether the management refused work to the 
concerned workman Sri Shambhu Mahato with effect from the 8th of November , 
1968 ? 

9 The case of the management is that the concerned workman Shri Shambhu 
Mahato was not in employment on the 8th November , 1968 the alleged date of 
refusal of work Ext M - 8 is Form H showing the names of the persons employed 
and qualified for membership of the Coal Mines Provident Fund during the 
quarter 1st April, 1956 , to 30th June, 1956 In this Ext M 6 the name of the con 
cerned workman Sri Shambhu Mahato appears at serial No 11 This has been 
Ailed by the management with the intention to show that the concerned workman 
had been member of the Fund in the said quarter April to June, 1956 and that he 
was under employment of the management then 

10 Ext M - 7 is the Form V relating to Annual Returns of the CMP Fund 
contributions submitted to the Coal Mines Provident Fund Commissioner for the

21. The evidence discussed above goes to show beyond any shadow of doubt 
that Sri Shambhu Mahato , the concerned workman was not in employment of the 
management of Bokaro Jharia Colliery on the alleged date of refusal of work i. e . , 
8th November , 1968 . The question of refusal of work to him on the 8th November, 
1968, therefore, does not arise. Hence my finding is that the management did 
not refuse work to Sri Shambhu Mahato , General Mazdür with effect from the 
8th November , 1968, and he is therefore , not entitled to any relief .

AWARD 
This is a reference under Section 10 ( 1) ( d ) of the Industrial Dispute Act. 

2 . Dr. T . E , R . Chetty joined his duties on 1st December 1935 as a Medical Officer 
in Newton Chickly Collieries Ltd . Hospital under an appointment letter dated 9th 
November, 1935 He worked for 32 years tili 24th November , 1967. His allegation 
is that during his tenure he carned a good name for his service in public at large 
and was awarded several certifcates in appreciation of his service by the then 
management. 

3 . Briefly stated his case is this . Dr. Chetty was called by the present Resident 
Director , Sri Chauchani, on 11th , 12th and 14th October, 1967 to his bungalow . 
Constant pressure and threats were used by him to make Dr. Chetty ręsign his 
post as Medical Officer , Dr Chetty brought this to the notice of the Managing 
Director of the Colliery staying at Jharla in Bihar by a registered letter dated 
14th October, 1987 but received no reply . On 19th October, 1967 Dr. Chetty sent 
a letter to the Resident Director, Newton Chickly Collieries , Parasla , mentioning 
the facts of undue pressure and coercion exercised by him on the petitioner , Dr. 
Chęty , on 11th , 12th and 14th October , 1967 to obtain his resignation . On getting 
this letter the Resident Director , Sri Chanchani, sent for Dr. Chetty on 19th October , 
1967 in his office and , behind closed doors , again used pressure and threats com 
pelling Dr. Chetty to resign against his will. Dr. Chetty succumbed to the pressure 
and threats and the Director succeeded in securing Dr, Chetty s resignation on 


Sec . 3 ( ii) ] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA ; AUGUST 22, 1970 / SRAVANA 31, 1892 368 


19th October, 1967 as dictated by the Director himself and against the Dr, Chetty s 
will, Dr. Chetty brought these facts to the notice of the Managing Dierctor at 
Jharia on 21st October, 1967 under a registered ietter with A / D along with a 
copy of letter on 19th October , 1967 addressed to the Resident Director but no 
reply was received . Again on 22nd November, 1967, Dr. Chetty wrote a letter to 
the Resident Director through the Agent of the Col.lery withdrawing the resigna 
tion letter of 19th October, 1967 as it had been taken under duress , coercion and 
undue influence and as no letter of acceptance had been received . The letter of 
acceptance of the resignation was actually issued on 23rd November 1987 after 
this letter of Dr. Chetty , The Agent was directed to inform him that the petitioner s 
resignation had been accepted and that the charge should be handed over to Dr. 
S . N . Mathur. The charge was actually handed over on 24th November, 1967. Dr. 
Chetty then raised an Industrial dispute before the Assistant Labour Commissioner 
( Central) , Chhindwara , Parasia . The dispute was taken up on 16th March , 1988 
between the parties by A . L . C ( C ), Chhindwara at Parasia and failure report was. 
submitted to the Government on 18th March , 1988 . On 14th June, 1968 , Dr, Chetty 
was iniormed by the Government of India that they did not consider the dispute fit 
for reference for adjudication to the industriai tribunal. Dr. Chetty applied for 
review and reconsideration and the Government of India after reconsideration 
referred the dispute to this wunal on 7th August , 1969 for adjudication under 
Sec . 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the Industrial Disputes Act. 


4 . Dr. Chetty claims that he is entitled to be reinstated , to be treated as con 
tinuing in service and to get full wages with all benefits for the idle period from 
25th November , 1967. 

5 . By notification N . 5 / 20 / 68 -LRII dated 7th August, 1969 the following matter 
in dispute was originaily referred to this Tribunal for adjudication : 
" Whether the management of Messrs Newton Chickly Colliery , Post Office 

Parasla , Madhya Pradesh has obtained resignation from Shri T . E R . 
Chelty , Medical Officer fraudulently on the 19th October , 1967 ? Il 

so, to what relief is Shri T . E . R . Chetty entitled ?" 
The schedule containing the matter in dispute was later on amended by Notifica 
tion No . 5 / 20 / 68 -LRII dated 20th December 1969 is as foliows : 
" Whether the management of Messrs Newton Chickly Colliery, Post Office 

Parasia , Madhya Pradesh has obtained resignation from Shri T . E . R . 
Chetty , Medical Officer under coercion or duress on the 19th Cctober , 

1967 ? If so, to what relief is Shri T . E . R . Chetty entitled ? " 
6 . The employers reply that the reference is bad and mişconceived in law on 
the grounds that a " Medical Omcer " is not a " workman " under the Industriai 
Disputes Act, 1hat the dispute had not assumed the character of an industrial 
dispute as it related to a single individual and that Sec, 2A of the Industrial Dis 
putes Act is ultra vires of the I. D . Act . On merits , the employer s case is that 
complaints carne to the management from time to time that Dr. Chetty was in the 
habit of charging fees from the employees and also the cost o medicines which , 
in fact , had been puichased on Company s account. This conduct irritated some 
of the employees and Dr. Chetty had been a victim of assault in 1947 -48 ky spine 
of the workers . Inspite of it Dr. Chetty did not improve his ways and an Industrial 
dispute was raised hy the Union before the Regional Labour Commissioner (Central) 
Jabalpur and the management had to submit a settlement incorporating the fol 
lowing terms : 
" Agreed that the practice of charging fees by the Colliery doctor for attend 

ing the workmen residing in Colliery quarters will be stopped forth 
will . The managerr.ent will see that the Doctor does not refuse to 
attend the serious cases of workmen patients in their respective 
quarters without sufficient reason . 


The management changed hands in 1965. Stray reports then , according to the 
employers , reached the management in respect of Dr. Chetty charging fees for 
treatment and cost of medicines supplied at Company s account. The management 
did not take therr . seriously at Arst. In 1967 more complaints of like nature were 
received against Dr. Chetty . The resident Director then sent for Dr. Chetty and 
told him that in the best Interest of industrial peace the Director had to frame 
charges against Dr. Chetty in pursuance of the compialnts and that it was for Dr 
Chetty to decide whether he would face the charges or resign , Dr. Chetty took a 
week s time to consider the matter . After due deliberation , he decided to submit 
and suhinitted his resignation . The resignation was accepted on 23rd November , 
1967 more than a month after its submission and the acceptance was communicated 


3686 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : AUGUST 22, 1970 / SRAVANA 31, 1892 [Part II 


to Dr. Chetty. According to the employers the resignation van given rith a free 
will after due consideration of the two alternatives given to him . Dr. Chetty had 
no complaint against the management after he was relieved on 23rd November , 
1967 till January , 1968. The employers case is that Dr. Chetty wanted a sizeable 
sum towards gratuity from the management and the manage ncnt was not pre 
pared to accede to his exorbitant demand and that Dr. Chetty raised this bogey 
of fraud , coercion and threats to pressurise the management even though there 
was 11 ) threat or pressure of any kind . 

7 . The issue for decision by this Tribunal is contained in the amended schedule 
of the reference order and is as follows : 
" Whether the management of Messrs Newton Chickli Coliery , Post Office 

Prasia , Madhya Pradesh has obtained resignation from Shri T . E . R 
Chetty, Medical Officer under coercion or duress on the 19th October , 

1967 ? If so , to what relief is Shri T . E . R . Chetty entitled ?" 
No other issue was framed by my predecessor. 
Finding : - 

8 . In the written statement of the employers it was also pleaded that a " Medical 
Officer " is not a " workman " under the Industrial Disputes Act and that the 
reference was bad and misconceived in law . This point was not seriously con 
vassed before mt in arguments But since it is a question of jurisdiction I would 
briefly deal with it . 


9 . A " workman " has been defined in Sec 2 (s ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, 
The definition runs as follows : 
Worl-man " means any person ( including an apprentice ) employed in anv 

industry to do any skilled or unskilled manual, supervisory , verhnical 
or clerical work for hire or reward , whether the terms of emplo ; 1,10nt 
bon expressed or implied , and for the purpose of giny proceedirig under 
this Act in relation to an industrial dispute , inclures any such person 
who has been dismissed , discharged or retrent hand in conne tirn with 
Ut as a reronsequence of that dispute, or in a r7jni sal discharge 
or retroachment has led to that dispute , ut joi : clude any 

such pri on 
Il who is . ubject to the Army Act, 1950 (16 of 1950 ) or the Air Force 

Art 1950 ( 45 of 1950 ) or the Navy (Discipline ) Ac , 1934 ( 34 of 

1934 ) ; Oi 
(11) who is employed in the police service crês úl officer or other 

emplovee oi a prison ; or 
( iii ) who is employed mainly in a managerial or administrutive capacity : or 
( iv ) who , being employed in a supervisory capacity , draws wages exceedino 

five hundred rupees per mensem or exercises, either by the nature 
of the duties attached to the office or by reason of the powers voted 

in him , functions mainly of a managerial nature ." 
Clearly , a person doing technical work for hire or reward is covered by the definition 
of a workman . A doctor possesses knowledge of human anatomy and acquires this 
knowledge and skill after years of hard labour. His function includes diagnosis 
and progliosis . Clearly , his work is of a highly technical nature . No layman can 
perform this work . A doctor has thus to possess knowledge of a specialised 
character . The definition specifically includes in the class of " workman " a ferson 
employed in any industry to do any technical work for hire or reward What 
ever doubts there were formerly have now been removed be the insertion of the 
word " technical" in the definition of " workman ” . 

10 . There is consequently not the slightest ieason tn doubt that a cortor " is a 
" workman " . The reterence cannot be said to be bad or misconceived in lavi. 

11. On merits, the question referred to this Tribunal is very simple anijs 
whether the management has obtained resignation frnm Dr. T E . P Chet y Medical 
Officer under cocrrion or duress on 19th Octcber , 1967 and if r2 . to what ielief 
he is entitled . 

12 . On this point. the evidence on behalf of the app i an eorsists of coin 
statement and Ex. W / 1 to W / 31. It is not mentioned in the written stalcmnt of 
the applicant what words were actua ly used hy the Resirlo Director when he 
is alleged to have obtained the resignant n of the Doctor. Pia ni the part of

llle application headed as facts of the case only says that when Dr. Chetty was

tor at Jharia mentioning all the facts about the undue criminal pressure being

help . css situation . It says that the petitioner was called in the Resident Director s

closed doors and had been given an ultimatum " for dire consequences " . Dr. 
Chetty s rejoinder says regarding para 3 ( i ) of the employer s written statement 
that the Resident Director called Dr. Chetty to his bungalow on 11th , 12th , and 14th

quences, 


13 The written statement of the cmployers, on the olher hand , says that when 
the Director received complaints about Dr, Chetty s charging fees for visits and

The written statement of the employers clearly says that no threat or cucrcion 
was used and that D . , Chetty was only given the option to resign or to face an 
enquiry .

went away and on 19th October , 1967 sent a letter to the Resident Director not to 
force him to resign under threats and requested him to retire him with gratuity

When Dr. Chetty said that he had appealed to the Managing Director also on 14th

15 . So, the only threat which appears from the statement of Dr. Chetty is that 
if he did not resign he would have to face bad consequences and would have to

he feed a true copy was written under dictation , Ex . W / 30 purports to be a 
copy of a letter written by Dr. Chetty to the Managing Director at Jharia dated 
14th October , 1967. The original has been filed by the management and is Ex, E / 2 . 
In this again there is no mention of actual words used by the Resident Director 
and all that is said is that the Resident Director advised Dr Chetty to resign the 
job and that pressure to this effect had been brought to bear on him . No threat 
or coercion was specifically mentioned in this letter apart from the use of the 
word " pressure" . It is also significant to note that in the copy Ex , W730 , 12th 
and 14th October have been inserted in ink although the copy is a typed one . 
The originai Ex . E / 2 admitted by Dr. Chetty as correct aves not contain the dates 
12th and 14th October after 11th October, 1967. This insertion of these two 
dates in ink show that Dr Chetty has no regard for truth and does not hesitate 
to make ingertions of dates in copies which are not in the original. It is not 
that these dates are lirelevant or immaterial to the enquiry , for the copy purports 
to say that on 12th and 14th also the Resident Director had advised Dr , Chetty to 
resign the job . Clearly then , it is not possible to rely on the statement of Dr. 
Chetty and to say on the basis of his statement that he resigned under duress and 
coercion 


16 . Ex . W / 1, which is not admitted by the employers , purports to be a true 
copy of the letter sent on 19th October, 1967 to the Resident Director. Apart from 
the statement of Dr. Chctty himself that such a letter was actually sent, there is 
nothing to show that such a letter was actually sent. It also mentions that there 
was a talk on 12th and 14th while the original of Ex . W / 30 letter sent to the 
Managing Director at Jharla on 14th soon after the incident contains only one 
datc — the 11th . The alleged incidents of 12th and 14th now sought to be intro 
duced are clearly and afterthought and a lie . 

17 . Ex . W / 2 purports to be a letter sent to the Managing Director at Jharia 
on 21st October, 1967. In the first place , there is nothing io show that this letter 
ever reached Managing Director at Jharia or was even sent except the statement 
of Dr. Chetty that he sent it by registered post by Post Box address with A / D . 
The acknowledgment card was admittedly not recelved back . Sri Dabral says that 
a registered letter sent by post box address is never delivered and the original of 
Ex , W / 2 would never be received by the Managing Director even if it was scnt. 
Any way , it purports to be a letter written by Dr. Chetty himself and the allega 
tions in that letter would not prove that any threat or coercion was actually used 
by the Resident Director to obtain Dr. Chetty s resignation . The letter itself 
Speaks only of heavy pressure and masked threats and not an open threat. Evi 
dently , Dr. Chetty has all along been taking the option of resignation or facing 
enquiry as threat, coercion and pressure , Sri Dabral rightly contends that Dr. 
Chetty would certainly take the option of facing an enquiry as pressure , But it 
was, in fact , an honest and straight forward course on the part of the Director 
to give Dr. Chetty the option of resignation or facing of an enquiry on the com 
plaints of the workers reaching the Resident Director . Ex . W / 2 , even if it is a 
true copy of letter actually sent by Dr. Chetty , would trot, therefore, prove any 
threat or coercion , Even Ex . W / 2 does not specifically mention that the resignation 
letter was written at the dictation of the Resident Director . 

18 . Ex , W / 3 purports to be a letter of withdrawal of the alleged resignation 
and is dated 22nd November , 1967. It does not relate to any alleged threats or 
coercion on or before the date of resignation . Ex . W / 4 is merely an acceptance 
of the resignation and is dated 23rd November , 1967. Ex , W / 5 and W / 6 are copies 
of wetters alleged to have been sent on 3rd January and 19th January . They are 
letters sent long after the resignation by Dr, Chetty himself and do not prove 
that any threat or coercion was used to obtain Dr. Chetty s resignation . 


19 . Ex . W / 7 purports to be a letter to the Asstt. Labour Commissioner to tako 
action in the matter according to law to meet the ends of justice . It is dated 29th 
January , 1968. Ex. W / 3 purports to be a telegram from Dr Chętly to Sri Jaswant 
Bhai Worah praying for justice and mercy and is dated 2nd February, 1968. Ex . 
W / 9 purports to be a letter dated 10th February, 1968 saying that the matter has 
been discussed between Dr. Chctty and Sri Chanchani and if he had to say any 
thing he was welcome to see Sri Chanchani any time in the next week . Ex . W / 10 
purports to be a letter from the Assistant Labour Commissioner to the Dy . Secre 
tary of the Government of India apprising him of the failure of conciliation , Ex . 
W / 14 purports to be a letter from Dr. Chetty to the Government of India through 
the Chief Labour Commissioner for referring the dispute to industrial tribunal. 


dec. 3(1 )] THE GAZETTE OP INDIA : AUGUST 22, 1970 /SRAVANA 31, 1892 3689 


Ex , W / 11 purports to be a copy of the minutes proceedings of the Pench Medical 
Association , Parasla regarding Dr. Chetty s case. Ex . W / 12 purports to be a copy 
of the communication from the Honorary Secretary Pench Medical Association to 
Dr. Chetty regarding the managemnt s letter to the Pench Medical Association . 
Ex. W / 13 purports to be a copy of Dr. Chętly s to the Honorary Secretary , Pench 
Medical Association . Ex . W / 15 purports to be a copy of certificate given by Sri 
K S . Mathur, Manager of Newton Chickii Colliery to Dr. Chetty in 1964. Ex . 
W / 16 , W / 17, W / 18 , W / 19, W / 20 , W / 21, W / 22 , W / 23 . W / 24 , W / 25 , W / 26 and 
W / 27 purport to be copies of testlinonials speaking of the wok of Dr. Chetty , 
Ex . W / 28 purports to be an appointmeni letter of Dr. Chetty dated 9th November , 
1935 . Ex . W / 29 purports to be a copy of letter to the Manager informing 
Dr, Chetty of the appointment of Dr . S . N . Mathur as a part - time Doctor to help 
him and lighten his work . Ex . W / 31 is only a certifcate of the Post-master 
saying that three registered letters were booked at Parasia , These documents 
do not prove the allegation of the use of threats or coercion to obtain Dr. Chetty s 
Tesignation . 

20 Ex E / 3 , the original letter of resignation , is alleged by Dr. Chetty to have 
been written at the dictation of the Resident Director. Ex . E / 3 runs as follows: 

" The Director , 
Newton Chickli Collleries, Ltd ., 

Parasia . 
Respected Sir , 

I am putting the resignation today, taking one month s period for this , 
I am leaving every thing on you for kind consideration as I have put 32 years 

service in this concern from 1935 , and I want to remain in good book 
with you always. 

Thanking you , 
With regards, 

Yours faithfully , 
( Sd.) T. E. R . Chelty . 

M . O . 

Dt. 19 - 10- 07” 
These are the exact words given in para 4 of the application made to the Govern 
ment of India , Ministry of Labour, through the Chief Labour Commissioner, New 
Delhi dated 16th June, 1969 ( Ex , W / 14 ). I ! the resignation had been written in 
the office of the Resident Director behind closed doors under his dictation , one 
fails to understund why and how Dr. Chetty would have a copy of that resignation 
letter . Without a copy he cannot givo the exact words including the punctuation , 
In fact, we would find that the words " Thanking you " and " With regards" have 
been written in two different lines in Ex , W / 15 exactly in the game manner as 
are in Ex , E / 3 , There is nothing on record to show that the Resident Director 
permitted Dr. Chetty to keep an exact copy of the resignation letter dictated by 
him . Nor does it stand to reason that he would give , or permit Dr, Chetty to make 
for himself, an exact copy of the resignaton letter dictated under threat and 
coercion as alleged by Dr. Chetty . Nor is the language likely to be what it is in 
Ex , E / 3 if the letter has been actually dictated by the Resident 
Director. If the letter was being dictated by the Resident Director 
himself there is no reason why he should have desired to wait for one 
month before its becoming effective . Clearly Dr, Chetty is telling a lie when he 
says that the letter of resignation was written at the dictation of the Resident 
Director. Shri Chanchani, the Resident Director , has come into the witness box 
himself as E . W . 1 and has categorically denied that it was written under his dicta 
tion or that it was written under duress or coercion , He has clearly stated that 
when the complaints became more frequent, he called Dr. Chetty and told him 
about the complaints and gave him the option that he could either face an enquiry 
or resign voluntarily I do not see any reason to disbelieve Sri Chanchani s sworn 
testimony on the point. Ir the intention had been to obtain resignation under 
coercion or threat , it would not have been difficult for the Resident Director to obtain 
complaints against Dr. Chetty and they would have been obtained . Sri Chanchani 
clearly says that the occasion for an enquiry did not arise because Dr , Chetty chose 
to resign and submitted his resignation letter. It is incorrert to say that Sri 
Chanchani had made up his mind to dispense with the services of Dr. Chetly and 
arrive at a finding against Dr, Chetty in the enquiry even if it had been held . There 
is nothing on record to show that Sri Chanchani would have cven made the enquiry 
himself or had inade up his mind as to why would be an enquiry officer , Shri

Chanchani was not asked about it . He had clearly given an opportunity to 
Dr. Chetty to resign fornalīy or face an enquiry as stated by Sri Chanchani and no 
threat or coercion is proved to have been used by Sri Chanchani in obtaining the 
resignation . Dr. Chetty s statement regarding threat and coercion is clear y false 
And unbelieveable . It is true that Dr Chetty had obtained good testimonials , but 
they do not show that he did not submit a voluntarily resignation , whatever their 
value may have been if there had been an occasion for enquiry by the management 
against Dr, Chetty . 


21. On a consideration of the entire evidence I find that resignation was not 
obtained under duress and coercion . Dr, Chetty is consequentiy not entitled to any 
Ielief . The reference is decided accordingly . No order as to costs . Let this award 
be sent to the Central Government,

का० प्रा० 2788. - - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
विषयों के बारे में मेसर्स वेस्टर्न कजोरा कोलियरीज ( प्राइवेट लिमिटेड , डाकघर रानीगंज , जिला 
बर्दवान की पश्चिमी कजोरा कोयला खान के प्रबन्धतंत्र से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के 
बीच एक औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करना वांछनीय 
समझती है ; 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 10 को 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा 
उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 - क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक अधिक रण ,

एल० प्रो० 2796...--कर्मचारी राज्य बीमा अधिनियप , 1948 ( 1 48 का 34 ) की 
धारा 88 द्वारा प्रदत शक्तियां का प्रयोग करते हए केन्द्रीय मरकार, समुचित सरकार होने के नाते , 
एतद्द्वारा भारत मरकार विस्तारण केन्द्र ( प्रौद्योगिक विकास और कम्पनी कार्य मंत्रालय ), 151 , 
पटेल रोड, कोयम्बटूर के उन कर्मचारियों को , जिनके नाम नीचे संलग्न अनुसूची में वर्णित है, उक्त 
अधिनियम के प्रवर्तन से , निम्नलिखित शर्ती पर, इस अधिसूचना के प्रकाशन की तारीख से एक वर्ष 
की कालावधि के लिये छूट देती है, अर्थात् : -- 

1. कि उ युक्त विस्तारण केन्द्र , जहां कि कर्मचारी नियोजित हैं , छूट प्राप्त कर्मचारियों 

के नामों और पदाभिधानों को दर्शित करने वाला एक रजिस्टर बनाए रखेगा ; 
2. कि इस छूट के होते हुए भी , कर्मचारी उक्त अधिनियम के अधीन ऐसे फायदे प्राप्त 

करते रहेगे जिनके लिए वे उस तारीख से पूर्व जिम से इस अधिसूचना द्वारा प्रदत्त 
छूट प्रवृत्त होती है, संदत्त अभिदायों के आधार पर हकदार हो गए हों ।

copy of the said arbitration agreement :

का० प्रा० 2798. -- यतः प्रारक्षित पूल कर्मकार, मद्रास डाक श्रम बोर्ड , मद्रास के लिए 
प्रशासनिक निकाय सूचीकृत डाक कर्मकार, मद्रास के लिए प्रशासनिक निकाय और भारतीय 
खाद्य निगम , मद्रास से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कमकारों, जिनका प्रतिनिधित्व , मद्रास 
हार्वर वर्कर्स यूनियन, मद्रास और मद्रास पोर्ट एंड डाक वर्कर्स प्रोग्रेसिव यूनियन , मद्रास करते हैं , 
के बीच एक औद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः उक्त नियोजकों और उनके कमकारों ने प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम, 1947 ( 1947 
का 14 ) की धारा 10 - 2 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन एक लिखित करार द्वारा उक्त विवाद को 
माध्यस्थम् के लिए निर्देशित करने का करार कर लिया है और उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10- क की 
उपधारा ( 3) के अधीन उक्त माध्यस्थम् करार की एक प्रति केन्द्रीय सरकार के भेजी है ; 

प्रतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10 -क की उपधारा ( 3) के अनुसरण में , केन्द्रीय सरकार 
उक्त माध्यस्थम करार को , जो उसे 27 जुलाई, 1970 को प्राप्त हुआ था , एसद्वारा प्रकाशित 
करती है ।

पक्षकारों के बीच निम्नलिखित प्रोद्यागिक विवाद को एतद्द्वारा श्री टी० बेंकटाद्री, उच्च न्यायालय 
के सेवानिवृत्त न्यायाधीश, प्लाट 207 , अन्ना नगर, मद्रास -40 के माध्यस्थम् के लिए निर्देशित करने का 
करार किया जाता है : 

( 1) विनिर्दिष्ट विवाद प्रस्त विषय : 
1. क्या (1) मद्रास लाफ श्रम बोर्ड के प्रशासनिक निकायों और ( 1) भारतीय खाद्य निगम 

के संबन्धित कर्मकारों की , जो मद्रास पत्तन के कार्य करते है, महापत्तनों पर पत्तन और 
डाक कर्मकारों के लिए केन्द्रीय मजदूरी बोर्ड की सिफारिशों को ध्यान में रखते हुए , 
उपय क्तता सम्बन्धित नियोजकों की गणना के अन् सार की जानी चाहिए या मद्रास 
हार्वर वर्कर्स यूनियन और मद्रास पोर्ट एंड डाक वर्कर्स प्रोग्रेसिव यूनियन की गणना के 

अनुसारः कर्मकार किस अनुपोष के, यदि कोई हो , हकदार हैं ? 
2. क्या डाक श्रम बोई , मद्रास के प्रशासनिक निकायों के अधीन कर्मकारों और भारतीय खाद्य 

निगम के कर्मकारों को , जो मद्रास पत्तन में कार्य करते हैं, मास में 26 दिन ( साप्ताहिक 
अवकाशों और संदाय सहित अवकाश को अपवजित करके ) की पूर्ण मजदूरी को , 
जिसमें सभी संघटक सम्मिलित है, मांग , महापत्तनो पर पत्तन और डाक कर्मकारों के 
लिए केन्द्रीय मजदूरी बोर्ड की सिफारिशों के अनुसार न्यायोचित्य है : 

कर्मकार किस अनुतोष के, यदि कोश हो , हकदार है ? 
टिप्पण : किसी भी विवाद्यक के अन्तर्गत ऊपर विवाचक 1 और 2 में वर्णित प्राधिकारियों द्वारा 

नियोजित प्राकस्मिक कर्मकार नहीं पाएंगें । 
( ॥ ) विवाद के पक्षकारों का ब्यौरा , नियोजिकों का प्रतिनिधित्व करने वाले

MINISTRY OF INDUSTRIAL DEVELOPMENT, INTERNAL TRADE 

( Department of Industrial Development ) 

New Delhi, the 14th August 1970 
2799 . -Whereas by the notification of the Government of India in the late 
Mini5 . 5 of Ind is rial Development, Internal Trade and Company Affairs (Depart 
incnt of Industrial Development) No. S . O 711, dated the 18th February , 1970 , the 
Central Government has, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 3 of the 
Commissions of Inquiry Act, 1952 (60 of 1652 ) (hereinafter referred to as the said 
Act ) , appointed a Commission of Inquiry (hereinafter referred to as the said 
Commission ) to inquire into and report on various matters of public inportance 
as set out in the terms of reference specified in the said notification ; 

And whereas the Central Government is of the opinion that having regard 
to the nature of the inquiry to be made and other circumstances of the case , the 
provisions of su5 - sections ( 2 ) , ( 3 ) , ( 4 ) and ( 5 ) of section 5 of the said Act should 
be made applicable to the said Commission ; 

___ Now, thereforc. in exercise of the powers conferred by sub - section ( 1 ) of 
section 5 of the said Act, the Central Government hereby directs that the pro 
visions of sub - sections ( 2 ), ( 3 ) , ( 4 ) and ( 5 ) of section 5 of the said Act shall apply 
to the said Commission . 

[ No. 6/ 1/ 70 - Adm. Cell. ] 
K . M . RAJPAL , Undrr Secy .

क ० प्रा० 2799. - --यत: भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व औद्योगिक विकास , आन्तरिक व्यापार और 
कम्पनी कार्य मंत्रालय ( प्रौद्योगिक विकास विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का० प्रा० 711 तारीख 18 
फरवरी, 1970 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने जांच आयोग अधिनियम , 1952 ( 1952 का 60 ) (जिमे 
इममें इसके पश्चात् उक्त अधिनियम कहा गया है ) की धारा 3 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हए , उक्त अधिमूचा में निर्दिष्ट निर्देश निबन्धनो में यथा उपणित विभिन्न लोक महत्व के विषयों में 
जांच करने और रिपोर्ट देने के लिए एक जांच प्रायोग ( जिमे इममें इसके पश्चात उक्त प्रायोग कहा 
गया है ) नियुक्ति किया है ; 


__ और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि की जाने वाली जांच के स्वरूप को और मामले की अन्य 
परिस्थितियों को ध्यान में रखते हुए, उवन अधिनियम , की धारा 5 की उपधाराओं ( 2) , ( 3) , ( 4 ) 
और ( 5 ) के उपबन्ध उक्त प्रायोग को लाग किए जाने चाहिए ; 


अतः अब उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 5 की उपधारा ( : ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते

New Delhi, the 5th August 1970 
$ . O . 2801. — In purguance of sub - regulation 5 of the Indian Standards Institution 
(Certification Marks) Regulations, 1955 , as amended from time to time, and consequent upon 
publication of IS : 4032- - 1968 Methods of chemical analysis for hydraulic cement , it is, hereby , 

notified that IS : 1298- - 1958 Methods of test for determination of frce line in portland cer.cnt , 
details of which were published under notification number S . O . 1438 dated 16 June 1959 , in the 
Gazetteof India, Part II , Section 3 - - Sub - Section ( i ) dated 27 June 1959. has been carcelled . 


[ No. CMD/ r3 :7 ]

विनियम 1955 के उपविनियम 5 के अनुसार और Is : 4032 - 1968 जलपुष्ट सीमेण्ट की 
रसायनिक विश्लेषण पद्धतियों के प्रकाशन के फलस्वरूप यह सूचित किया जाता है कि Is : 1298 
1958 पोर्टलैण्ड सीमेण्ट में मुक्त चूने की मात्रा निकालने की परीक्षण पबतियां, जिसके ब्यौरे अधि

MINISTRY OF INDUSTRIAL DEVELOPMENT AND INTERNAL TRADE 

( Department of Industrial Development ) 

ORDER 

New Delhi, the 7th August 1970 
S . O . 2806 . - IDRA / 18G /69, - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 18G , 
and section 25, of the Industries ( Development and Regulation ) Act , 1961 ( 65 of 
1981) , and of all other powers enabling it in this behalf, the Central Government 
hereby makes the following Order further to amend the Cement Control Order , 
1967 , namely , 

1. (1 ) This Order may be called the Cement Control (Second Amendment ) 
Order , 1970 . 

( 2 ) It shall come into force at once . 
2. In clause 8 of the Cement Control Order, 1987 , in item ( a ) , for the figures 
" 152. 13 " the figures " 154. 13 " shall be substituted . 

1 [ No . F . 1 - 39/ 70 - Cem.] 

G . RAMANATHAN , Under Secy . 
DEPARTMENT OF COMPANY AFFAIRS 

New Delhi , the 6th August 1970 
S . O ., 2807 . - In pursuance of clause ( b ) of regulation 20 of the Cost and Works 
Accountants Regulations, 1959, the Central Government hereby makes the 
following further amendment in the notification of the Government of India in 
the late Ministry of Commerce and Industry ( Department of Company Law 
Administration ) No . S . R. O . 2118, dated the 19th September, 1959, narmely: - - 

In the said notification , after item 27, the following items shall be inserted . 
namely : 
" 28 First Year Examination of the Five Year Integrated Course in 

Engineering conducted by the Indian Institute of Technology . 
29 First Year Examination of the Five Year Integrated Course in Engineer 

ing of any University costituted by law in India . 


conducted by the Board of Technical Education of any State 
Government or of Government of Union Territory of Delhi." 

_ [ No. F . 10 / 17 / 65 - I. G . C . ] 

___ M . C . VARMA, Under Secy . 
कमपनी कार्य विभाग 
सागत और संकर्म लेखापाल 

नई दिल्ली , 6 अगस्त 1970 
का०मा० 2807. लागत और संकर्म लेखापाल विनियम , 1959 के विनियम 20 के खण्ड ( ब ) 
के अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व वाणिज्य और उद्योग मंत्रालय ( कंपनी विधि 
प्रशासन विभाग ) की अधिसूचनां सं० का० नि० प्रा० 2118 तारीख 19 सितम्बर, 1959 में एतद्वारा 
और मागे निम्नलिखित संशोधन करती है, अर्थात् - 

उक्त प्रधिसूचना में , मद 27 के पश्चात्, निम्नलिखित मदें प्रन्सः स्थापित की जाएंगी, अर्थात्: --- 
" 28. भारतीय तकनीकी संख्या द्वारा संचालित , इंजीनियरी में पंज वर्षीय समाकलित 

पाठ्यक्रम में प्रयम वर्ग की परीक्षा । 
29. भारत में विधि द्वारा गठित किसी विश्विद्यालय की इंजीनियरी में पंचवर्षीय समा 

कालित पाठ्यक्रम में प्रयम वर्ग की परीक्षा । 
30. किसी राज्य सरकार या दिल्ली के संघ राज्य क्षेत्र को सरकार के तकनीकी शिक्षा 
बोर्ड द्वारा संचालित , सिविल, वैचुत या यांत्रिकी इसीनियरी में डिप्लोमा परीक्षा । " 

[ एफ० सं० 10/ 17/ 65- माई जी सी ] 

एम० सी० वर्मा, प्रवर सचिव ।
